The grep counts include all "true"/"false" occurrences in the JSON, which includes both the "successes" array and possibly other fields. Each task has 50 episodes, and there are 10 tasks per suite = 500 episodes total. The "successes" field has true/false entries, but the JSON also has other true/false entries potentially. Let me think about this more carefully.

Actually, in the JSON structure, each per_task entry has: sum_rewards (numbers), max_rewards (numbers), successes (true/false), video_paths (strings). So the only true/false values in the JSON are in the "successes" arrays. 


Now I'm verifying the counts match the expected 500 total episodes per suite. The baseline_plus spatial variant shows 408 successes and 92 failures for an 81.6% success rate, baseline_plus object has 444 successes and 56 failures at 88.8%, baseline_std spatial shows 330 successes and 170 failures at 66.0%, and baseline_std object appears to be cut off but follows the same pattern.
